# Gap table: reptile

Made by `tools/views/gaps.py`. The 1200 species most recorded worldwide that borrow this view cover 93.5% of its 3,091,009 research-grade records; the 20 most recorded in Alachua County cover 85.7% of the county's 9,642. Counts are iNaturalist research-grade records through 2024. The view's own measured animal is not excluded: it shows as `m` where it was measured.

Row grades: `m` every trait of the row measured in that species; `g` every trait measured or carried by a rule; `i` at least one trait neither. The detail columns give the state of each trait.

Rows and their traits:

- **The whole view**: receptor_classes, lambda_max_nm, acuity_cpd, fov_h_deg, pupil_shape, fovea
